{D833}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_badzra satwa mA yA dza la gu h+ya sarba A dar+sha nA ma tan+t+ra/_bod skad du/_rdo rje sems dpa'i sgyu 'phrul dra ba gsang ba thams cad kyi me long zhes bya ba'i rgyud/_/o~M rnam par snang mdzad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/hU~M mi bskyod pa la phyag 
[\[132b.2\]]'tshal lo/_/a~M snang ba mtha' yas la phyag 'tshal lo/_/de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'chang /_/rgyal ba kun gyi gsang ba'i bdag_/kun gyis dbang bskur gter 'dzin pas/_/dgyes shing spyan gdangs gus bcas pas/_/rgyal ba kun la bstod bskor nas/_/
[\[132b.3\]]spyan sngar gus 'dug 'di skad gsol/_/e ma'o sangs rgyas kun gyi gsung /_/rtog pa kun gyi rgyal po ste/_/cho ga rgyas par bstan pa'i rgyud/_/dri med zab mo brtag dka' ba/_/'dul ba mdo sde chos mngon dang /_/dam tshig bsgrub dang grub pa dang /_/sku gsung 
[\[132b.4\]]thugs kyi cho ga'i rgyud/_/ma lus gtan la 'bebs pa 'di/_/rdo rje sems dpa' ji ltar lags/_/sgyu 'phrul gang gis bstan pa de/_/dra ba'i rgyud de gang phyir bstan/_/lung ni gang phyir brjod pa nyid/_/gang phyir spyi zhes bya bar bshad/_/gsang ba zhes ni ji lta 
[\[132b.5\]]bu/_/snying po'i don ni ci yi phyir/_/de kho nyid ces ji ltar brjod/_/nges pa zhes pa ci yi phyir/_/mtshan 'di gang zag gang la gsungs/_/mtshan gyi dri ba dris pa'o/_/thun mong khyad par tshul nyid kyi/_/ston pa zhes ni ci yi phyir/_/gnas dang 'khor ni ji lta bu/_/
[\[132b.6\]]dus dang sdud pa ji lta bu/_/mi bskyod pa ni ji ltar bya/_/rnam par snang mdzad ji ltar brjod/_/rin chen 'byung ldan gang gi phyir/_/snang ba mtha' yas gang gi phyir/_/don yod grub pa ji lta bu/_/spyan dang nam mkha'i dbyings ma dang /_/gos dkar mo dang mA ma kI/_/
[\[132b.7\]]sgrol ma zhes pa gang gi phyir/_/sa yi snying po ci yi phyir/_/nam snying spyan ras gzigs dbang dang /_/phyag na rdo rje gang phyir brjod/_/sgeg mo phreng dang glu gar ci/_/byams dang sgrib sel kun bzang dang /_/'jam dpal zhes pa ci yi phyir/_/dus bzhi bdug spos me tog 
[\[133a\]]
[\[133a.1\]]dang /_/mar me dri chab gang phyir brjod/_/rnam rgyal gshin rje gshed po dang /_/rta mgrin bdud rtsi 'khyil pa ci/_/lcags kyu zhags pa lcags sgrog dang /_/dril bu zhes pa gang phyir brjod/_/yid dang chos bzang gnyis ci phyir/_/gtso bos bdag la nges par 
[\[133a.2\]]gsungs/_/gang phyir thun mong ma yin pa'i/_/nang dang gsang ba'i gleng gzhi las/_/ston pa bstan pa gnas dang 'khor/_/dus dang sdud pa ji ltar brjod/_/grags pa thun mong gleng gzhi las/_/ston pa 'khor gnas dus ji ltar/_/bstan pa sdud pa ji lta bu/_/skyes bu 
[\[133a.3\]]chen pos bshad du gsol/_/'di ni gleng gzhi'i dri ba tshigs su bcad pa'o/_/thun mong thun mong ma yin pa'i/_/gleng bslang gnyis su ci phyir 'gyur/_/gang gis gang la gleng bslang zhing /_/thabs dang 'bras bu ci yin ston/_/thun mong thun mong ma yin pa'i/_/sems lnga 
[\[133a.4\]]ye shes lnga yi rim/_/gang dang 'brel pa bshad du gsol/_/'di ni gleng bslang ba'i tshigs su bcad pa'o/_/skyes bu chen po ston pa'i gtso/_/sgyu 'phrul dra bar mnga' brnyes shing /_/phun sum tshogs pa chos kyi rje/_/gzi brjid phung po phun sum tshogs/_/blta bya gang 
[\[133a.5\]]la ci yi phyir/_/gang gis rtogs pas ji ltar 'gyur/_/rtogs dang ma rtogs khyad par ci/_/rtogs pa'i sgo ni du ru gsungs/_/gtan tshigs tshul dang 'bras bu ci/_/'di ni lta ba'i dri ba'o/_/thun mong spyod pa ci yi phyir/_/thun mong thun mong gang ci'i phyir/_/shes rab 
[\[133a.6\]]tshogs dang thugs rje dang /_/rtogs dang phyogs gcig spyod pa dang /_/'brel dang rig 'dzin spyod pa ci/_/gang gis gang la ji ltar spyad/_/de yi 'bras bu gang phyir 'gyur/_/gang du de dang mi ldan pa'i/_/spyad pa'i skyon nyid bdag la gsungs/_/'di ni spyod 
[\[133a.7\]]pa'i khyad par gyi dri ba'o/_/thun mong spyi dang khyad par gyi/_/rang bzhin nyid dang lhun grub kyi/_/dkyil 'khor zhes pa gang ci'i phyir/_/de nyid phyi nang gsang ba dang /_/'dul thabs gnyis su ji ltar 'gyur/_/ting 'dzin dkyil 'khor rim gsum gang /_/lhag pa 
[\[133b\]]
[\[133b.1\]]ting 'dzin zhes bya ba/_/gnyis la sku gsung thugs sbyor dang /_/phyi nang gsang ba'i rim pa ci/_/gzugs brnyan dkyil 'khor ji lta bu/_/gang phyir gzugs brnyan zhes brjod cing /_/gang gi gzugs brnyan gang la snang /_/phyi nang gsang bar ji ltar 'gyur/_/
[\[133b.2\]]lhag pa zhes pa gang phyir te/_/gzugs brnyan zhes pa ji lta bu/_/gnyis dang gsum dang drug bstan cing /_/don dam kun rdzob byang chub sems/_/phyi nang gsang ba ji ltar sbyor/_/tshogs kyi dkyil 'khor gnyis ci zhig_/gang gis gang du gang tshogs nas/_/ji ltar 
[\[133b.3\]]spyad pas ci zhig 'grub/_/'di dag bstan pa'i dgongs pa ci/_/gnas skabs gang dang 'brel par 'gyur/_/yan lag rtsa ba gang dang 'brel/_/dkyil dang 'khor du ji ltar brtag_/gsungs shig skyes bu mchog chen po/_/gang dang nges pa ma rnyed par/_/kun gyi 
[\[133b.4\]]spyod par ma gyur pa/_/legs par gsungs shig rgyal mchog che/_/'di ni dkyil 'khor gyi dri ba dris pa'o/_/gang gis gang la gang dag tu/_/ji ltar dbang bskur phun sum tshogs/_/bdag gis ji ltar shes par bgyi/_/gang dang gang du ji ltar 'brel/_/skyon 
[\[133b.5\]]dang yon tan ji lta bu/_/'bras bu rim pa bshad du gsol/_/'di ni 'jug pa'i cho ga du/_/las kyi rim pa ji lta bu/_/rdo rje sems dpa' bde chen po/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba mngon rdzogs pa'i/_/thabs dang shes rab gnyis med pas/_/bdag gi the tshom bsal du gsol/_/'di 
[\[133b.6\]]ni dbang gi don mtha' dag la dri ba dris pa'o/_/gang la gang gis brten pa yi/_/dam tshig ces pa ci yi phyir/_/rtsa ba dam tshig du zhig bstan/_/gang phyir rtsa bar brjod par bya/_/yan lag du zhig bstan pa gsungs/_/'di dag lam dang bsnyen bsgrub dang /_/gang zag gang 
[\[133b.7\]]don ji ltar 'brel/_/bsrungs dang 'das pa'i skyon yon ci/_/phyi nang gsang bar ji ltar gsungs/_/lta dang spyod dang sgrub pa dang /_/shes bya ting 'dzin dam tshig tu/_/grub dang mi spong bsrung ci zhig_/rgyal mchog skyes bus bshad du gsol/_/thun mong thun mong ma yin pa'i/_/
[\[134a\]]
[\[134a.1\]]mchog dang mchog gyur ma yin pa'i/_/bsgrub pa'i khyad par ji lta bu/_/gang gis gang du ji ltar bsgrub/_/dgug dang bcing dang gso dang bsad/_/dbye dang bskrad dang pham rgyal ci/_/gter dang mig rnon rkang mgyogs dang /_/rig 'dzin go 'phang ji ltar sgrub/_/
[\[134a.2\]]rgyal ba mchog gis bshad du gsol/_/'di sgrub pa'i dri ba dris pa'o/_/phrin las rim pa gang ci zhig_/gang gis gang du brtsam par bya/_/thab dang rdzas dang phur bu ci/_/ting 'dzin 'dug stangs hom khung ci/_/gdul bya rim pa gang ci zhig_/khyad par rim pa ji ltar bshad/_/
[\[134a.3\]]'bras bu yon tan ji ltar 'gyur/_/gsungs shig gsungs shig rgyal mchog gtso/_/the tshom legs par chod par mdzod/_/'di ni phrin las kyi dri ba dris pa'o/_/rmad 'byung skyes bu gang zhig gis/_/dang po lam tshul ji lta bu/_/phyi nang gsang ba'i lam ci phyir/_/stong pa'i rnal 
[\[134a.4\]]'byor snying rje che/_/phyag rgya cig dang spros bcas ci/_/rnam smin dbang sgyur phyag rgya dang /_/lhun gyis grub pa'i mtshan nyid ci/_/mthar phyin rnam pa du zhig gsungs/_/phyi nang gsang ba mthar phyin pa'i/_/'bras bu bdag gzhan don nyid ci/_/nyi shu rtsa lnga'i don ci ltar/_/gang

[\[134b.5\]]mchog_/nyon cig da ni sems dpa' bshad/_/chos rnams ma lus gnyis med pa'i/_/rang rig byang chub sems nyid de/_/tha dad med pa'i rang bzhin nyid/_/rdo rje zhes ni bshad pa yin/_/rang bzhin mtha' thug med pa'i chos/_/thabs dang shes rab 'brel pa yis/_/

[\[135a.6\]]pa/_/dngos po gsum gyis khyab gyur pa/_/yongs su rdzogs pa'i rgyu yin no/_/thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i/_/de bzhin nyid du rdo rjes rig_/bdag dang gzhan don nus pa rdzogs/_/'bras bu'i de kho na nyid do/_/gang phyir dngos gnyis tha dad par/_/log par

[\[135b.1\]]nges pas nges pa gsum du bstan/_/gang du theg pa bzhi gsungs pa/_/'gro ba 'dren phyir ma nges pa/_/ro gcig theg par nges gnas pa/_/nges pa'i theg pa bla na med/_/rang bzhin ma bcos rang byung las/_/bya byed ngang gis bral ba yi/_/sna tshogs sgyu ma'i dkyil 'khor

[\[135b.3\]]sgyu 'phrul dra ba yi/_/lam gnyis 'brel par mngon bsgoms pas/_/thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i/_/bde chen thig le mngon snang ba/_/'jug dang grol ba'i lam nyid de/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i lam du brjod/_/rang bzhin yongs kyis khyab pa yi/_/dngos dbang thabs

[\[135b.5\]]bskal pa grangs med sngon rol nas/_/shes rab sbyangs dang snying rje ldan/_/dad dang brtson 'grus dam tshig dang /_/cho gas dag par sbyangs pa yis/_/dbang po rab kyi spyod yul yin/_/rdo rje gsang ba'i theg pa 'di/_/ngo bo nyid dang byin brlabs dang /_/mtshon dang rdo rjer grub pa 
[\[135b.6\]]ste/_/rdo rje theg pa bde ba che/_/rang bzhin ngan dang dman pa dang /_/phyin ci log dang mi mos dang /_/dbang dang dam tshig med pa la/_/gsang phyir gsang ba'i theg par gsungs/_/thabs dang bral ba'i slob dpon gyis/_/snod dang bral ba'i slob ma la/_/gsang ba'i snying po

[\[136a\]]
[\[136a.1\]]don/_/thun mong ma yin 'khor dag la/_/phyogs bcu rgyal ba rdo rje 'chang /_/dkyil 'khor mngon du phyung ba yi/_/rdo rje pad+ma 'brel pa yis/_/byang chub sems kyi dbang bskur nas/_/thun mong thun mong ma yin pa'i/_/nyon mongs lta dang shes bya 
[\[136a.2\]]rnams/_/skad cig yud la bsal byas te/_/thun mong khyad par gsang ba yi/_/lam gnyis nges par ston pas na/_/ston pa zhes ni brjod pa yin/_/de yi gnas mchog bshad bya ba/_/'byung dang bzhugs dang rten pa dang /_/mtshon pa byin gyis brlabs phyir ro/_/
[\[136a.3\]]thabs dang lam gyi khyad par gyis/_/kun bdag rdo rje 'chang chen nyid/_/gtsang ma ris dag spangs pa yi/_/'og min gnas mchog chen po der/_/yang dag sangs rgyas der sangs rgyas/_/skal med skye bo gdul ba'i phyir/_/sprul pa po ni 'dir sangs rgyas/_/sangs rgyas kun 
[\[136a.4\]]gyi bzhugs pa'i gnas/_/gtsang ma rigs dag rnam spangs pa/_/'jig rten las kyis mthar gyur pa/_/'og min 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang /_/phyogs bcur rgya yongs ma chad pa'i/_/yon tan khyad par spags pa na/_/gsang ba yum gyi b+ha ga la/_/bcu gsum sa yi 
[\[136a.5\]]dbang phyug ldan/_/rdo rje 'chang chen dpag med dang /_/til 'bru'i tshul du rab tu bzhugs/_/chos rnams kun gyi de bzhin nyid/_/thog ma tha ma dbus med pa'i/_/rang bzhin don gyi 'og min ni/_/rgyal ba kun gyi pho brang phyir/_/'das dang da ltar ma byon pa'i/_/rgyal ba 
[\[136a.6\]]kun gyi bsten pa'i gnas/_/de dag nye bar mtshon pa yi/_/yid dang mtshan ma las gyur pa'i/_/bsgoms dang bris dang byas la sogs/_/de dang 'dra ba'i gzhal yas khang /_/rgyal ba sngags dang byin brlabs kyis/_/der gnas don phyir mtshon pa'i gnas/_/thun mong thun mong ma 
[\[136a.7\]]yin pa'i/_/bsod nams ye shes khyad par can/_/bsags pa sna tshogs las byung ba'i/_/rdzogs longs spyod pa chen po ni/_/gtsang ma ris dag spangs pa yi/_/'og min chen po'i gnas mchog na/_/rigs bdag phyag rgya lhun grub sku/_/gcig dang du ma rnams spangs pa/_/
[\[136b\]]
[\[136b.1\]]sangs rgyas kun gyi spyi gzugs te/_/gdod nas theg mchog mdzod nyid phyir/_/sgrib pa mtha' spangs gdul bya la/_/skad cig gcig gis spang bar mdzad/_/chos rnams sna tshogs ji snyed pa/_/dngos po tha dad med phyir zlum/_/khyab dang spags dang tshad med dang /_/ye shes bzhi phyir 
[\[136b.2\]]'khor lo bstan/_/chos dbyings 'du 'bral med pa'i phyir/_/mu khyud zlum por kun nas bskor/_/sku gsung thugs dang byin brlabs dang /_/thugs rjes ma khyab med pa'i phyir/_/yon tan rgyas pa'i dbyibs su bstan/_/rang 'byung ye shes mtha' bral bas/_/phyogs bcur 'gro 
[\[136b.3\]]'ong mi gnas pa'i/_/tshad dang bral ba'i sems kyi phyir/_/gzhi rtsa tshad med 'khor lor bstan/_/gdod nas byin brlabs ngo bo'i phyir/_/ma bsags lhun rdzogs mdzod nyid de/_/'gro ba re 'dod skong ba'i phyir/_/rin chen 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang /_/sna tshogs dngos dang dngos 
[\[136b.4\]]med pa'i/_/chos kun ngo bor rang gnas te/_/bdag gzhan gzung 'dzin bral ba'i phyir/_/phyi dang nang bral sgrib pa med/_/thun mong la brten thabs kyi 'bras/_/ye shes thabs kyis 'phags pa nyid/_/gdul bya'i blo la mngon snang bas/_/glo 'bur bzhi ni lhag par mdzes/_/

[\[136b.6\]]babs rgyu 'bras bcu drug bstan/_/zhen dang spong ba'i lam bral bas/_/'dod yon ye shes lnga rol te/_/bdag mchod kun mnyes las byung bas/_/'dod yon lha mos 'khrigs par bstan/_/thabs dang shes rab ldan pa yis/_/ma chags thabs dang ldan pa dang /_/che ba'i 
[\[136b.7\]]yon tan lhun rdzogs pas/_/nyi zla pad+ma rin po che/_/las dang thugs rje smon lam gyis/_/byang chub sems dpa' mthu bo che/_/smon lam dbang gis de dag tu/_/thos pa rgya mtsho bsag phyir gnas/_/de tshe dus kyi tha bral yang /_/ngo bo dus bzhi mnyam pa'i dus/_/dus kyi 
[\[137a\]]
[\[137a.1\]]mtha' la mngon rtogs pas/_/thun mong min pa'i dus su bstan/_/de tshe phyogs bcu rgyal ba yis/_/bskal pa bye bar lung bstan pa/_/'dul ba'i dus bab gsang ba'i bdag_/lag na rdo rjes bsdu ba mdzad/_/dus gsum rgyal bas byin 
[\[137a.2\]]brlabs phyir/_/'khor gyi nang na skal mchog ldan/_/gsang ba'i rdo rje 'chang bsdus nas/_/'dul ba'i 'khor la bstan par mdzad/_/'das dang ma byon da ltar gyi/_/sku gsum sku gsung thugs nges pa'i/_/de bzhin nyid du thabs gang gis/_/gshegs shing 
[\[137a.3\]]gshegs dang gshegs gyur pa'i/_/thar pa gsum dang ye shes dang /_/pha rol phyin pa drug ldan pa/_/zhing khams yon tan bsam mi khyab/_/bye brag brgyad cu kun 'dus pa'i/_/rgyal ba rgya mtsho kun gyi mtshan/_/gang phyir rdo rje thugs chud pas/_/las dang 
[\[137a.4\]]spyod pa dka' thub dang /_/srid pa'i ri bos mi bskyod pas/_/de ni mi bskyod mchog tu gsungs/_/thabs dang shes rab 'brel pa yis/_/ji snyed sku gsung thugs snang ba/_/mos dang thabs kyi khyad par gyis/_/rnam par snang phyir snang mdzad mchog_/rgya ba kun gyi 
[\[137a.5\]]yon tan ni/_/tshogs rnams 'dus pa'i ngo bo ste/_/yon tan rin chen ltar 'byung bas/_/rin chen 'byung bar gsungs pa yin/_/dpag tu med pa'i gsung gi sgo/_/dbang po thabs kyi bye brag gis/_/don rtags gsung dang yi ge'i sgo/_/mtha' yas snang phyir 
[\[137a.6\]]mtha' yas mchog_/dpag med thugs rje mdzad pa yi/_/'gro 'ong med pa'i thugs rje yis/_/'gro ba skyob pa'i don mdzad pas/_/don grub ces ni rnam par bstan/_/rig pa'i rgyal po 'di dag kyang /_/tha mal lnga nyid gnas dag cing /_/de las thabs des mngon byas 
[\[137a.7\]]pas/_/der dbang sgyur te nyid snang ba/_/rgyal po nyid du brjod pa yin/_/steng 'og gnyis las mngon gyur pa'i/_/sbyor dang 'ju 'dul rlung gi rim/_/gang de thabs las mngon gyur pa'i/_/bde ba'i gzugs dang tshor ba dang /_/'du shes dang ni 'du byed dang /_/rnam shes zag 
[\[137b\]]
[\[137b.1\]]med bde ba'i ros/_/dbang sgyur ye shes rgyal por bshad/_/gang phyir snang dang bsgoms dang bris/_/phyag rgya rtsa bcud thig le'i mdog_/dkar dang dmar dang ser ba dang /_/sngo dang ljang dang rims ldan pa'o/_/rgyal ba kun gyi bde skyid cing /_/spyod dang de las 'byung ba'i yum/_/

[\[137b.3\]]yis/_/'khor ba dang ni mya ngan 'das/_/gnyis med gzigs pa spyan gyi mchog_/zag med stong pa'i klong chen gyis/_/skyed dang sdud dang gnas pa'i phyir/_/mtha' dbus bral ba'i de bzhin nyid/_/nam mkha'i dbyings zhes gang brjod pa'o/_/thun mong thun mong ma yin pa'i/_/
[\[137b.4\]]las dang lta ba nyon mongs dang /_/shes bya'i dri ma mtha' yas pa/_/rtogs pas sbyong zhing sreg pas na/_/gos dkar ma ru brjod pa yin/_/gang phyir las dang ting 'dzin dang /_/ye shes thugs rje bar med chus/_/gang dag 'khrud dang tshim byed pas/_/chu ni mA ma kI ru 
[\[137b.5\]]bshad/_/gang phyir rtogs dang thabs dag dang /_/ting 'dzin thabs kyi rlung dag gis/_/bgrod pa med pa'i zhing khams su/_/sgrol bas sgrol ma nyid du bshad/_/snying po rang 'byung ye shes nyid/_/kun brten gnas dang khyab pa'i phyir/_/byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin

[\[138a\]]
[\[138a.1\]]par ni/_/thabs dang shes rab 'brel pa las/_/mtshon dang rtogs dang bsgoms pa yis/_/lha mo bzhir ni mngon snang ba/_/byang chub sems dang bde ba dang /_/thabs kyi bde bas rab sgeg pas/_/lha mo 'di ni sgeg mor bshad/_/grol dang

[\[138a.7\]]pa'i dkyil 'khor la/_/me tog thig ler bder bkod pas/_/rdo rje me tog bla na med/_/phyi nang sems la gnas bcas pa'i/_/mun pa bsal phyir shes rab dang /_/ye shes snang ba bla na med/_/rdo rje gsal ba'i mar me ma/_/phyi nang dkyil 'khor ma lus la/_/rang

[\[138b.1\]]'byung ting 'dzin bde ba yis/_/thams cad khyab par gnas pa'i phyir/_/khyab byed rdo rje dri chab ma/_/de bzhin nyid la dgongs pa yis/_/'khor ba'i chos rnams ma lus pa/_/byang chub snying por tshar bcad pas/_/rnam par rgyal ba zhes su brjod/_/gzung 'dzin bral

[\[139a.1\]]ste/_/bar ma ar+tha byang chub sems/_/nam mkha'i ye shes thugs kyi klong /_/ma na ma ta dran rtog yid/_/rnam shes rgyu ba'i dran rtog rnams/_/ye shes nam mkha'i gsal 'dod pa/_/dran rtog rig pa 'khor du gnas/_/zag med rnam shes bsam mi

[\[139b.1\]]'dul mdzad pa/_/[{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]drag pos gdung ba sel/_/dmyal ba sdug bsngal chos kyi rgyal/_/gdul ba'i sprul sku ngo bor shar/_/la la lha las 'pho ba dang /_/la lar lhums zhugs bltam pa dang /_/dka' thub bdud btul sangs rgyas nas/_/'khor lo bskor

[\[141b.3\]]dngos med dngos po yul 'das la/_/'khor dang mya ngan 'das pa yi/_/ngo bo sna tshogs rang snang ba/_/gnyis med thabs chen rdo rje nyid/_/e ma'o gnyis med bde chen las/_/sna tshogs gnas dang longs spyod dang /_/rigs dang kha dog tshe tshad dang /_/bde sdug dbang po bye 
[\[141b.4\]]brag dag_/rang bzhin sna tshogs rnam par 'phrul/_/sgyu ma chu zla 'ja' tshon ltar/_/byang chub sems kyi ngo bor gnas/_/las dang rkyen gyis gang snang ba/_/rang rang zhen pa'i tha dad kyis/_/dbang po khyad par sna tshogs pa'i/_/rtog pa'i nus pa sna tshogs mthong /_/rang bzhin 
[\[141b.5\]]gzhi rtsa med pa'i dbyings/_/lhan cig skyes dang kun brtags pa'i/_/ma mthong log zhen rtas pa yis/_/'khrul 'khor log pa'i stobs su snang /_/'khor ba ma bsal dag pa yi/_/zhing dang spyod yul longs spyod dang /_/ting 'dzin dbang stobs rdzu 'phrul dang /_/mngon 
[\[141b.6\]]shes ye shes dpag med pa'i/_/sna tshogs sku dang gsung thugs dang /_/yon tan phrin las rang snang ba/_/bdag gzhan lhun gyis grub pa'i don/_/sna tshogs yid bzhin nor bu ltar/_/ma bsags lhun gyis grub pa'i sku/_/sna tshogs kun dang dbyer med pa'i/_/kun la 
[\[141b.7\]]yid bzhin mthun par snang /_/kun kyang rang 'byung byang chub sems/_/bden pa dbyer med don du snang /_/gang phyir nam mkha'i rang bzhin ltar/_/gang la de ltar snang ba las/_/yang dag gcig tu gzung du med/_/ma bcos rang 'byung zhi ba nyid/_/rnam par dag 
[\[142a\]]
[\[142a.1\]]pa'i snying po ste/_/'khor ba nyid na mya ngan 'das/_/rnam par dag pa'i dbyings su mkhyen/_/sgro dang skur pa kun bral ba'i/_/thams cad rang snang snying po las/_/sna tshogs ma lus rnam par 'phrul/_/dngos dang dngos po med min zhing /_/gang la de nyid 
[\[142a.2\]]yul 'das pa/_/nam mkha' lta bur dpag med pa/_/phyogs ris mtha' dbus rgya dpag med/_/gang gis gang la khyab pa ltar/_/mkha' sogs rdul bral 'byung ba'i bcud/_/zag pa med pa'i snying po nyid/_/lus can kun gyi dbang po dang /_/dngas mar 'brel 
[\[142a.3\]]bar gang snang yang /_/mthong pa log pa'i stobs kyis sgyur/_/bdag gzhan med pa'i rang snang yin/_/ma lus thams cad dbyer med pa'i/_/thabs dang shes rab bcud lnga'i rgyun/_/longs spyod thabs su bcas pa nyid/_/'gro ba kun la khyab par gnas/_/gang yang 
[\[142a.4\]]thabs dang shes rab kyi/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i rang bzhin nyid/_/rang bzhin dbyer med byang chub sems/_/bde ba chen po'i ngo bo nyid/_/'das dang da ltar ma byon gyi/_/rgyal ba rig 'dzin mtha' yas dang /_/ma lus kun gyis rtogs bya ba/_/phyogs med khyab pa'i ngo bor gnas/_/
[\[142a.5\]]gang snang ma lus de bzhin nyid/_/ma bcos ma byas zhi ba ste/_/gcig dang du ma las 'das pa/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin nyid/_/mtha' bral nam mkha'i rang bzhin ltar/_/ma lus gnas pa de dag kyang /_/rtogs pa'i khyad par dbang po yi/_/mthong tshul dag 
[\[142a.6\]]dang ma dag gis/_/rang bzhin yul du ma gyur pas/_/phyi nang gsang ba la sogs pa'i/_/dbang dang man ngag 'brel ba yis/_/de nyid mthong rtogs don phyir bshad/_/gang phyir rdo rje slob dpon nyid/_/yon tan yongs rdzogs nus pa can/_/dad dang brtson 'grus 
[\[142a.7\]]brtul zhugs can/_/shes rab khyad par gang ldan la/_/dbang dang dam tshig man ngag dang /_/rgyu rkyen 'brel pa'i nus pa yi/_/bde ba gsal ba mi rtog pa/_/thabs kyis phun sum tshogs par 'gyur/_/gsang ba'i gsang chen snying po nyid/_/sku gsung thugs kyi dngos 
[\[142b\]]
[\[142b.1\]]grub ste/_/phyi nang bdag gzhan ma lus pa/_/thams cad dbyer med de bzhin nyid/_/thabs bral kun la rgyas btab pas/_/mun pa'i tshogs su gyur pa yin/_/mun snang dug dang gser 'gyur rtsi/_/thabs mkhas rang bzhin nges 'gyur ltar/_/gang de'i lta spyod ting 'dzin gyis/_/
[\[142b.2\]]de ma thag tu de bzhin 'gyur/_/dbyer med nam mkha'i rang bzhin du/_/kha dog dbyibs dang rdzas la sogs/_/ma lus tha dad med pa ltar/_/thabs mkhas ma lus de bzhin 'gyur/_/tha mal pa dang log pa dang /_/dka' thub can dang rkyen rtogs dang /_/phyi nang can dang tha dad 
[\[142b.3\]]can/_/gtsang sme can dang phyogs rtogs nyid/_/mnyam nyid lta las gang 'das pa/_/ngo bo nyid dang thabs dag dang /_/dbang po la sogs log pa yis/_/de nyid gnod pa'i stobs su 'gyur/_/gang la gang gis zin pa'i nad/_/thabs dang brtson 'grus legs pa yis/_/de ltar 
[\[142b.4\]]brtson pa'i khyad par gyis/_/de nyid ma spangs rnam par dag_/gang phyir de nyid nges pa la/_/rtogs dang ma rtogs khyad par gyis/_/tha dad chos su gang snang ba/_/dngos la de ltar gnas pa min/_/rdo rje skye med shes rab ste/_/me long thabs nyid sna tshogs snang /_/gnyis med 'brel 
[\[142b.5\]]pa'i mnyam pa nyid/_/ma lus dbyer med dam par bshad/_/de nas rtogs sgo bshad bya ba/_/gang la gang gi don med pas/_/lta dang spyod dang lam 'bras dag_/'di nyid don gnyer gzhan med pa'o/_/gang gis de dag de don du/_/tshig dang sngags rdzas dka' thub kyis/_/
[\[142b.6\]]gang bstan de yi byin brlabs kyis/_/bdag gzhan phan par gang 'dod pa'o/_/gang las gyur pa de zhig pas/_/lus sems 'byung ba'i bag la gnas/_/phyin cad phan gnod chos med pas/_/dka' thub 'bad rtsol mi dgos so/_/gang phyir byed pa'i rgyu bzhi dag_/mnyes 
[\[142b.7\]]dang dag pas bde bya'i phyir/_/las dang dka' thub drag shul gyis/_/grol zhing thar bar 'dod par snang /_/bden bzhi bcu drug tha dad lam/_/khams gsum mthong bsgom spong tshul gyis/_/rang don zag pa kun bral nas/_/rang don thar pa mya ngan 'das/_/rkyen rtogs 
[\[143a\]]
[\[143a.1\]]bag chags sad pa yis/_/'byung med bcu gnyis rang brtags nas/_/ma rig 'khrul 'khor rgyu 'bras zad/_/yid bzhin bde ba mchog gi sa/_/tha dad med pa'i rig pa'i sems/_/bden [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]khyad par cir bltas pa/_/gsal la mi rtog snying 
[\[143a.2\]]po nyid/_/bdag gzhan nus pa'i stobs su snang /_/chos rnams sgyu ma mig yor dang /_/brag ca chu zla 'dra ba yi/_/de nyid mtha' dbus bral ba ni/_/bden gnyis 'brel pa dbu ma'i tshul/_/rdo rje dam pa gsum bkod de/_/dngos po sgyu ma las grol nas/_/dag pa'i rten

[\[143a.5\]]par gyis/_/dbang po yul dang rnam shes dang /_/snang srid phyi nang snod bcud kyi/_/spyod yul ji ltar 'gyur ba kun/_/thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i/_/gsang ba'i thabs kyis rgyas btab pas/_/ma lus rdo rje nyid du 'gyur/_/snang srid dpa' bo thabs chen de/_/

[\[143b.1\]]de dag bltas pa'i khyad par gyis/_/rang re'i mos dang bsam pa dang /_/nus pa'i rim pa cir snang yang /_/nam mkha' dang ba'i rang bzhin las/_/nyi zla gza' skar sprin khu rlangs/_/rang 'byung rang dngas 'gyur med ltar/_/ji ltar snang ba'i chos rnams kun/_/dngos

[\[146a.4\]]dgur ldan pa'i/_/gcig las rgyas bsdus phye ba yis/_/'khor lo'i rtsa ba lha dag kyang /_/yab yum gnas dgod gsal bar bsgom/_/de dag lhag pa'i lha dgu dag_/yan lag tshul du spros te bsgom/_/'dul ba gnyis kyi rang bzhin gyis/_/mdzes pa'i tshul dang gdug 
[\[146a.5\]]pa 'dul/_/lha dang zhal phyag dbu grangs kyang /_/rtsa ba yan lag spros tshul gyis/_/dkyil 'khor grangs dang gang sbyar ba'i/_/rim par spros shing bsgom par bya/_/grangs dang grangs med tshul dag gis/_/dpag tshad rgyang grags mkha' dbyings su/_/zhi dang khro 
[\[146a.6\]]la sogs pa yi/_/sangs rgyas sprin tshogs sna tshogs bsgom/_/dang po'i bar dang tha ma'i bar/_/rig pa'i ting 'dzin gsal brtan nas/_/sku gsung thugs dang ldan pa yi/_/tshom bu rim par bsgom par bya/_/dgon pa'i rim pa'am nags tshal dang /_/chu 'gram 
[\[146a.7\]]ri rtse dur khrod dang /_/skyon bral yon tan rdzogs pa ste/_/dgra dang phyogs mtshams skyon med pa'i/_/brtag pa'i rim pa ldan pa yis/_/shis dang 'thad pa'i sa phyogs su/_/gzhal yas rim brtsegs mdzes ldan pa/_/rgyan dang bcas pa ldan byas la/_/ting 
[\[146b\]]
[\[146b.1\]]'dzin mnyam pa'i shes rab thabs/_/mi ldan gong ma'i 'od kyis spyad/_/bdag dang gzhan gyi nus pa yis/_/mnyam nas tshogs la 'jug par bya/_/yo byad lnga ldan thogs med par/_/cho ga'i rim pa ji bzhin brtsam/_/bro glu gar dang ting 'dzin bcas/_/bar 
[\[146b.2\]]chad med par bsgrub par bya/_/gal te 'dod pa ma rdzogs par/_/sgyu ma'i lus de ma brtan na/_/yid kyis rdo rje gsum gsal bas/_/rnam par smin pa'i rig 'dzin de/_/phyin cad 'jigs bral khyung bzhin no/_/de bas 'brel pa'i spyod ldan na/_/dbang sgyur zag med 
[\[146b.3\]]sa thob ste/_/brgya dang stong sogs 'jig bral zhing /_/gling dang 'jig rten brgya stong du/_/'dul ba'i cho 'phrul mtha' yas snang /_/'jigs pa med cing mi ldog sa/_/dpa' bo rdzogs pa'i rnal 'byor pas/_/gzhan don spyod pa'i khyad par dag_/'dul ba'i dbang 
[\[146b.4\]]ni sna tshogs kyis/_/rnam par brtse ba sna tshogs spro/_/'di ni rgyal ba thams cad kyi/_/shes rab phyi yi spyod pa ste/_/cho ga rgyas par bstan pa yin/_/de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba/_/nang du 'jug pa'i rim pa yi/_/spyod pa'i khyad par cho ga dag_/de nas rim pa 'di dag 
[\[146b.5\]]bshad/_/lus can 'gro ba thams cad la/_/tsakra bzhi dang sku gsung thugs/_/spor dang 'bar ma 'khyil pa dang /_/'brel dang ma 'brel khug pa dang /_/'dzin dang 'dzag dang gnas pa dang /_/de bzhin khyab dang 'khrul ba'i rtsa/_/gang du rgyu dang gnas 'dzin pas/_/rtsa zhes de la

[\[147a\]]
[\[147a.1\]]'khor lo ste/_/gsung gi 'khor lo ngag gi rten/_/rdzogs dang ma rdzogs gang mi gsal/_/sgra sbyor dag dang ma dag gyur/_/bde dang snyan dang mi snyan dang /_/bya dang dri za bung ba dang /_/chags pa'i sgra ni sna tshogs byed/_/mdog dmar lus khyab klu la sogs/_/u 
[\[147a.2\]]tha'i rlung dang 'grogs te gnas/_/d+harma tsakra srog gi rten/_/ma lus gzhi rtsa kun gyi srog_/rtsa bzhi yan lag brgyad dang ldan/_/dngas ma lnga 'dus kun gyi gtso/_/phyi rim rin chen sul mang 'dra/_/mi shigs thig le bde chen po/_/rin chen phung po za ma tog_/sna tshogs ye shes lnga yi

[\[147a.4\]]nyid rim pa gsum du gnas/_/rlung yang gcig gam gnyis yin te/_/de las bye brag dpag med pa'o/_/gnas pa dang ni rgyu ba'i rlung /_/gnas pa srog dang ye shes te/_/rgyu ba bzhi ni mngon par brjod/_/phyi nang can dang gyen thur can/_/de re'ang dngas snyigs khyad par gyis/_/

[\[147b\]]
[\[147b.1\]]ba'i sa bon can/_/rtse mo'i 'og mas reg pa'i khams/_/gtum mo lte 'og 'bar ba'i rtsa/_/skyes bu chags pa slong ba ltar/_/'di dag bde ba bskyed pa ste/_/gnas sa tshad dang bcas par gnas/_/de 'og g.yo ba rlung gi rten/_/'byed dang 'dzums pa'i las can nyid/_/

[\[147b.3\]]'dud pa g.yas g.yon te/_/bcu drug 'dus pa'i srog rtse gong /_/rtsa gsum mgrin par 'dus pa ni/_/sgra sbyor 'khor lo ngag gi gnad/_/de nas dngas ma'i thig le la/_/ma lus 'dus pa'i tshul du gnas/_/rig pa'i rtsa la bcud 'dus pas/_/thogs med

[\[148a\]]
[\[148a.1\]]lo rtsa bcud ldan/_/sa bon yi ge bde ba dang /_/khams lnga ye shes ro ldan zhing /_/stong dang bde ba'i thig le 'dres/_/rtag tu dran par gnas pa nyid/_/re re'ang de ltar gnas pa'i phyir/_/'khor lo rtsa bcud thig ler bcas/_/lha dang ye shes sku gsung

[\[148b.1\]]ma/_/rdol ma nyo tshong ma nyid dang /_/ma dang sgyug mo sring mo dang /_/mthar sgeg lus 'tshong ma la sogs/_/dngos grub skye ba'i zhing du bshad/_/bcu gnyis bcu bzhi bcu drug dang /_/[{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad bcud ldan rab yin te/_/nyi shu la sogs 'bring du bshad/_/rtsa lnga tha ma'i

[\[149b.1\]]bar snang /_/de dag lam la zhugs pa yi/_/phyi nang gsang ba ma lus pa'i/_/spyod pa'i khyad par thams cad kun/_/'dir ni rgyas par bstan pa yin/_/thabs dang shes rab ldan gyur pas/_/phyi nang gsang ba la sogs pa/_/skabs la bab pa'i rnal 'byor pa/_/gang yang rung

[\[150a.1\]]brtan g.yo ste/_/ji ltar snang grags thams cad kun/_/nges pa'i rang bzhin bzhi bcu gnyis/_/rang bzhin longs spyod rdzogs skur bshad/_/de nyid phyag rgya sna tshogs te/_/kun du sku dang gsung dang thugs/_/yon tan phrin las bsam yas

[\[150b.3\]]bkod pa phun sum tshogs par gnas/_/gang de zag med ye shes kyi/_/phyag dang zhal dang thabs ldan pas/_/zhal mthong rigs la dbang sgyur bas/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa chen por gyur/_/rgyal ba de dag ma lus pa'i/_/gsung mchog brjod du med pa nyid/_/nam mkha'i dbyings

[\[159b.1\]]rab 'thor kun du 'thor/_/'gul zhing ldeg pa de bzhin te/_/phyogs bcu dus bzhi gang ba yi/_/sgra bo che yang sgrogs par byed/_/phyogs bcu'i gdug pa ma lus pa/_/thul zhing brgyal nas myos gyur te/_/de dag bde bas bslang byas te/_/bde ba chen po dgyes pa'i

[\[160a\]]
[\[160a.1\]]mongs kun/_/tshig pa gsum du shes par bya/_/ye shes bde ba chen po nyid/_/'bar ba gsum du shes par bya/_/byang chub sems kyi bde ba yis/_/ma lus gang zhing khyab par gyur/_/thig le gong 'og steng 'og nyid/_/thig le gong ma nyid 'og tu/_/'og ma nyid ni

[\[160a.3\]]'byung bzhi rdo rje pad+ma nyid/_/reg pa 'dus pa'i bde ba nyid/_/re dogs rgyu ba chad pa nyid/_/de bzhin nyid du blta bar bya/_/thig le 'dres pa'i bde ba yis/_/sa yang bcu gsum khyab pa'i yid/_/ma lus mkha' ltar dri med pa'i/_/rang 'byung phyogs med kun du snang /_/thabs

[\[162b.1\]]le la/_/'joms pa'i rang bzhin gsal bar byed/_/nyams myong skye dang rnam pa med/_/spyod yul dag pas nang du bshad/_/zla ba nyi ma la brten te/_/sku gsung thugs kyi lam rgyud de/_/thig le g.yo dang 'bar ba dang /_/re dogs med dang rnam par dran/_/bde ba'i yan lag

[\[163a\]]
[\[163a.1\]]thig ler brten/_/nyi ma'i snying po lta ba ltar/_/bltas pas mthong ba med pa'i don/_/ma lus bde chen rgyan du snang /_/de nas gzugs brnyan dkyil 'khor bshad/_/'khor dang mya ngan 'das pa yi/_/ji snyed ma lus chos bstan pa/_/rang 'byung ye shes chen 
[\[163a.2\]]po ste/_/de nyid dngos po sna tshogs mchog_/ji snyed brtan g.yo snang ba'i chos/_/chen po rags pa gnas pa'i chos/_/ma lus kun la mthun snang ba/_/rang 'byung thabs kyi ngo bo nyid/_/de dag gdod nas ma skyes pa'i/_/rdo rje shes rab stong nyid de/_/gang yang dngos min ngo bo 
[\[163a.3\]]nyid/_/shes rab yum gyi rang bzhin no/_/ji snyed nam mkha'i mthar thug pa/_/rang 'byung byang chub sems kyi gzugs/_/ma lus kun gyi rang bzhin nyid/_/dpag med mthun par snang ba ste/_/me long dang ba'i rang bzhin nyid/_/sna tshogs gzugs dang 'brel pa bzhin/_/ma lus

[\[177a.1\]]ni/_/rang lus rten gyi don snang ba/_/khyab par rgyu ba'i tshul dag gis/_/dngas snyigs gnyis kyi tshul du gnas/_/rang lus rtog pa dngas snyigs dang /_/kha dog yan lag nying lag gis/_/bde ba'i ye shes lngar shes dang /_/bde ba chen po nyid du 
[\[177a.2\]]gsungs/_/khams lnga bcud las skyes pa yi/_/ma lus thig le'i rang bzhin te/_/ye shes lnga yi ro ldan pas/_/rgyal ba lnga ru yang dag gsungs/_/thabs dang shes rab rdo rje dril/_/nyi zla'i rim pa'i 'od gsal ba/_/sems lngas mkha' la gsal ba'i tshe/_/rten

[\[177b.1\]]'bring du rtsa ba lnga nyid dang /_/yan lag bcu ru shes par bya/_/shin tu rgyas pa'i dam tshig ni/_/spyir ni sum brgya drug cu ste/_/rang bzhin 'od gsal ba nyid dang /_/nyid kyi dkyil 'khor snang ba dang /_/dus bzhi rgyal mchog nyid dang ni/_/bsgrub med mnyam par

[\[187b.7\]]de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba/_/gsang ba'i lam la gang dga' bas/_/thun mong ma gyur gsang ba'i lam/_/rim pa nyid du bshad par bya/_/dad dang brtul zhugs che ba dang /_/theg pa chen por rigs nges shing /_/snying brtse dbang dang dam tshig ldan/_/sgrub la

[\[190b.3\]]drug gam/_/bcu gnyis bcu bzhi bcu drug gis/_/tshe la dbang sgyur zag med thob/_/de nas brgya phrag bcu drug gis/_/sku lnga lhun gyis grub par 'gyur/_/skye bo mdza' ba kun tshogs pa/_/rnal 'byor 'dus pa'i tshogs zhes bya/_/'byor pa'i longs spyod tshang ba ni/_/

[\[190b.5\]]pas/_/rig pa'i skyes bu thub pa med/_/lha mi cha byad 'dzin mod kyi/_/'byung lnga cho 'phrul dbang sgyur thob/_/zhing dang longs spyod dag pa yis/_/gong nas gong du mnyam par sbyor/_/rdo rje rig pa 'dzin gyi sku/_/zhing khams kun la gnas par byed/_/gal te mtshan mar zhen 
[\[190b.6\]]dbang gis/_/rtogs pa'i stobs ni chung gyur na/_/byang chub phyag rgya rdo rje sku/_/mtshan dang dpe byad la sogs pa/_/cha lugs mngon du byung na yang /_/zhi ba de nyid mthong ba min/_/gang du sbyar ba'i yum rnams kyang /_/thun mong lam dang skabs gnyis su/_/nus pa'i khyad 
[\[190b.7\]]par che chung gis/_/phrin las bzhi ru bshad pa ni/_/dkar mo bong chung skad che la/_/bzhin lus gtsug phud g.yas su 'khyil/_/yan lag phra 'jam skad gsal zhing /_/rlung khar lci bas zhi ba bya/_/zla dang yan lag mang ba dang /_/phra zhing bshan la gzangs su ring /_/
[\[191a\]]
[\[191a.1\]]bzhin lus ser zhing gru bzhir gnas/_/rnam 'phyangs sha dang nu ma rgyas/_/'phel ba'i dus su 'di dag gzung /_/bzhin mdog pad 'dra sog ka la/_/smin dkyus ring la stod smad phyed/_/lus dang chu rlung dri zhim zhing /_/bde ba ldan zhing yan lag med/_/
[\[191a.2\]]'di ni sdud pa'i mchog tu 'gyur/_/nag mo gru gsum rtsid mgo ma/_/khro gnyer byis pa'i lus ldan zhing /_/gtum zhing brlang tshig 'dzem pa med/_/gtsang sme'i rtog bral gsod par bshad/_/gang du nus pa brtan thob pa/_/de dag sa nas sar sbyong ba/_/rig pa'i skyes 
[\[191a.3\]]bu dam pa nyid/_/rnal 'byor rim pa'i lung ldan pa/_/ma skyes mnyam la dbang sgyur zhing /_/ma lus gsal bar mthar phyin pa'o/_/rang rig ye shes rgyud don phyir/_/rang snang rig pa mngon byas pa/_/rig 'dzin mchog ste lhun grub pa'o/_/ting 'dzin nus pa'i mthu 
[\[191a.4\]]med kyang /_/sa sbyong sems dpa' bskyed du med/_/bder gshegs nyid dang spyod yul gcig_/rig pa'i skyes bu'i lung thob pa'o/_/gal te 'byung ba'i lus 'jig na/_/bdag nyid sgo gsum lhar lta zhing /_/'byung ba bsags pa'i lus las grol/_/ting 'dzin phyag 
[\[191a.5\]]rgya chen por smin/_/khyung phrug sbubs dang bral ba ltar/_/ma lus phyag rgya sgyu ma'i mchog_/rnam par smin ces de la brjod/_/zag pa zad pa rdo rje lus/_/phyin cad 'gyur 'jig chos dang bral/_/bde ba chen po lung thob pas/_/zag bcas bslab pa'i chos dang 
[\[191a.6\]]bral/_/zhing la dbang sgyur sprul pa'i lus/_/ting 'dzin longs spyod dpag tu med/_/bsgrubs pa'i nus pa gang brtan pa/_/rdo rje lus gyur 'jig pa med/_/'jig pa lnga bral zag pa zad/_/dbang sgyur gsum la dbang thob pas/_/bde ba longs spyod dpag 
[\[191a.7\]]tu med/_/grangs med bskal par don byed cing /_/mthong bas de bzhin gnyis med rigs/_/rgyal tshab skyes bu sa sbyong ba/_/phyi nang snod bcud ma lus pa/_/nam mkha' lta bur dpag med cing /_/mtha' dbus bsam pa'i rgyun las 'das/_/'jigs 
[\[191b\]]
[\[191b.1\]]bral dbugs phyin mya ngan 'das/_/rang snang dpag med zhal mthong bas/_/gzhan du blta ba'i yul dang bral/_/ting 'dzin rig pa goms pa'i mthu/_/mtshan ma sdug po yul las 'das/_/'jig rten stong phrag dbang sgyur zhing /_/gnyis med phyag rgya chen po'i 
[\[191b.2\]]rigs/_/nam mkha' mthar thug med pa ltar/_/bsgoms dang nus pa'i rtsal stobs kyis/_/bag chags mtshan ma'i tshang tshing la/_/ting 'dzin rig pa'i me byung bas/_/zhing khams rgya bral dpag med du/_/ting 'dzin rig pas gang sbyangs pa/_/bsgoms dang ting 
[\[191b.3\]]'dzin rig pa yis/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa'i lung thob pa/_/phyag rgya chen po'i rig 'dzin yin/_/de nas rig pa'i rtsal rdzogs shing /_/ye shes lha mo rol dbang gis/_/mthar phyin rig pa'i skyes bu ste/_/zhing khams bye ba'i dbang thob cing /_/rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid kyi sras/_/dbang 
[\[191b.4\]]dang cho 'phrul kun thob pa/_/nam mkha'i mtha' ltar dgongs pas khyab/_/mnyam bzhag rjes thob khyad par gyis/_/rgyal ba'i lung mnod bka' la gnas/_/zhing khams dag par kun spyod kyang /_/rmad 'byung gsang ba'i rang bzhin la/_/spyod kyang lta ba'i rang bzhin gyis/_/
[\[191b.5\]]phyogs bral nam mkha' mi mthong bas/_/dbang dang dgongs pas mthar phyin 'gyur/_/dbang dang dam tshig brtul zhugs te/_/dang po bslab pa mthar phyin cing /_/thig le rdzogs par 'dod pa yis/_/gsang ba nang gi don la sbyar/_/thun mong bya ba mthar phyin nas/_/lus dang 
[\[191b.6\]]spyod yul bcas pa dang /_/lha yi rnal 'byor ma lus pa/_/bde chen thig le nyid du blta/_/lha dang bde bar mnyam sbyor bas/_/thig le bde bar snang ba nub/_/sku bzhi dkyil 'khor khams thig le/_/dngas ma lnga ru mnyam sbyor bas/_/ngo bo dang ni rkyen dag gis/_/thabs kyi 
[\[191b.7\]]rim pa bskul las shes/_/bde ba'i thig le nyams myong ba/_/mi rtog bde ba cha shas med/_/mi shigs thig le kun gyi bdag_/stong nyid 'brel pa'i ri mo can/_/tha mal bya ba kun bral ba/_/spyod pa nyid du bstan pa yin/_/thig le bzhu ba'i rang bzhin gyis/_/'dzag 'bar 
[\[192a\]]
[\[192a.1\]]thim gang bde ba ni/_/sa yang bcu gsum bde bas brgyan/_/lus kun khyab pa'i thig le shum/_/til 'bru bcud kyis khyab pa ltar/_/ma lus khyab pa'i ros gang ba/_/ma lus 'od dang snying po ltar/_/snang srid thig le chen po nyid/_/bde ba chen pos khyab 
[\[192a.2\]]par gsal/_/ma lus 'dus pa'i thig le ni/_/khams lnga 'dres pa'i snying po ste/_/mi shigs thig le bde ba'i bcud/_/ma lus bde ba'i rgyas btab pas/_/nam mkha'i ye shes cir yang gsal/_/dus gsum rgyal ba'i dgongs pa ste/_/thig le gcig gis kun khyab pas/_/
[\[192a.3\]]bde chen snying rjes gcig tu 'khyil/_/de nyid bde chen phyag rgya ste/_/nang gi phyag rgya gcig par bshad/_/de nyid nges par las rung bas/_/thig le bde chen dang bcas pa'i/_/bye brag brgyad cu ma lus par/_/so so'i thig ler sku 'phros shing /_/khams lnga ye shes ro dang 
[\[192a.4\]]bcas/_/sa bon thig le bde bas brgyan/_/mi rtog thig le thugs mchog sgra/_/rang lus smin nas snang bar spro/_/gang nas gang la spros gyur pa/_/de srid thig le'i phyag rgya ste/_/bde ba'i thig le grangs las 'das/_/ye shes lnga dang sku gsung thugs/_/mi phyed ro yis 
[\[192a.5\]]gang khyab pas/_/grangs 'das bde chen tshogs rdzogs nas/_/ma lus bde ba'i phyag rgyar snang /_/gang de de yis rgyas btab pas/_/de nyid bde chen thig ler smin/_/rnam pa phyi nang snyigs mar bcas/_/bde chen thig le'i ngo bor smin/_/snyigs ma 'byung ba'i lus 
[\[192a.6\]]las 'das/_/snying po bar chad med pa'i lus/_/thig les tha ma'i snod gang bas/_/ma lus bde ba chen por smin/_/de nas thabs dang ting 'dzin dang /_/rlung gi 'dren pa'i stobs ldan pa/_/rin chen lung gi thig ler zhugs/_/skad cig mngon shes lngar smin cing /_/
[\[192a.7\]]'gro ba ris dang thar par 'gyur/_/mi shigs a la thig le thim/_/'byung 'jug med pa'i rlung 'thob cing /_/'gyur 'jig med pa'i lus la gnas/_/'phel 'grib med pa'i bde ba nyid/_/snang ba thig le nyid du ste/_/thig le thung ngu a ru thim/_/gnyis med bde ba 
[\[192b\]]
[\[192b.1\]]cha shas bral/_/'di ni zag med rang byung ba/_/rkyen dang bral ba'i bde chen nyid/_/dper na me dang tsha ba'am/_/rgya mtsho dang ni rlan bzhin no/_/rlung dang thig le rig pa dang /_/tshe dang longs spyod 'byung ba dang /_/'dod lnga kun la dbang sgyur thob/_/bde ba'i phyag rgya 
[\[192b.2\]]chen po ni/_/ma lus zag med thig le ste/_/rgya bral rig pa'i thig le nyid/_/snyigs ma'i zag pa rlung zad pas/_/ting 'dzin bde chen thig ler gsal/_/rig pa bde chen ror gsal bas/_/ci mthong bde chen phyag rgya nyid/_/'du bas sgyur ba'i spyod yul lam/_/
[\[192b.3\]]mun 'gyur dug rtsi ji bzhin du/_/rgya dang bral ba'i zhing khams dag_/bde chen phyag rgya chen por 'gyur/_/gang dag bsgoms pa'i skyes bu ni/_/tha mal pa dang brkam chags pa/_/rnam pa'i spros pa ma lus pa/_/bsgoms dang ched du ma byas par/_/bde chen thig ler lhun 
[\[192b.4\]]gyis grub/_/dper na chu dang rlabs bzhin nam/_/rgya mtshor phyin pa'i 'du shes ltar/_/goms pas bde chen gsal mthar phyin/_/rkyen dang gnod pa ma lus pa/_/reg dang mthong thos dran pas kyang /_/bde chen thig le nyams su 'bar/_/bskal pa du mar gnas gyur 
[\[192b.5\]]cing /_/'byung ba lnga dang spyod yul kun/_/rang lus bde bar bsre ba 'grub/_/gsang ba'i lam la goms byed na/_/mtshon byed rig pa'i bde ba yis/_/lhan cig skyes pa'i dga' ba rtogs/_/mthar phyin lam gyi rim pa 'grub/_/dbang po dar zhing chags pa dang /_/bde 
[\[192b.6\]]chen mos pa'i rnal 'byor pas/_/e dang ba~M la gnas pa yi/_/byang chub rdo rje dril las 'byung /_/dngos po lhan cig skyes pa yis/_/ma lus bde bar rtogs byas la/_/phyi nang sbyor ba'i cho ga yis/_/gang zhig rtags dang ldan pa'i tshe/_/gsang ba'i phyag rgya bzhi ldan pas/_/
[\[192b.7\]]bde chen mchog la rol par bya/_/ye shes nyi zla mnyam ldan pas/_/re dogs chad pa'i thig le 'byung /_/lhung med gnas dang thim pa dang /_/bdag po gdung can phrad pa las/_/nyin mtshan bral ba'i snang ba ltar/_/dus gcig nyi zla shar ba'i tshe/_/ma lus bde bas khyab gyur 
[\[193a\]]
[\[193a.1\]]na/_/g.yo ba med pa'i thig le 'char/_/gal te nyams pa'i ltas byung na/_/thabs dang nyams dang rol pa yis/_/rim pa bzhin du bslab par bya/_/thabs dang shes rab gnyis med pa'i/_/nyi zla 'dres pa'i thig le nyid/_/dngos bral lhan cig skyes 
[\[193a.2\]]pa nyid/_/rgyal ba kun gyi phyag rgya yin/_/lhan cig skyes la gnas pa'i tshe/_/ma lus bde chen ro gcig pas/_/bde chen phyag rgya gcig tu gnas/_/rdo rje pad+ma'i thig le nyid/_/sa ya bcu gsum phrag gnyis rtsa/_/nyi zla 'dres pa'i gar thabs kyis/_/so so bde ba'i pho brang 
[\[193a.3\]]du/_/thig les gang bar spros pa'i tshe/_/sku gsung thugs dang ye shes lnga/_/nyams myong ro yis rgyas btab bo/_/lus kyi bde ba mthar phyin nas/_/rgyu rkyen rten 'brel las 'das pa'i/_/rig pa'i bde ba la gnas bya/_/gal te nyams na bsam pa'am/_/blta dang mnyan 
[\[193a.4\]]pa la sogs pas/_/bar chad med par bsgom par bya/_/rig pa bde bar gang smin pas/_/sgo gsum bde ba chen por smin/_/spyod yul rlung dang thig ler bcas/_/goms pa'i thabs la dbang sgyur bas/_/rten dang brten par bcas pa yi/_/thig le rlung la dbang sgyur 
[\[193a.5\]]zhing /_/lha mo mi mo gnod sbyin mo/_/mkha' 'gro rnams la dbang byed nus/_/thig le dbab dang 'dzin pa dang /_/zlog dang sbyor la dbang sgyur te/_/gal te mkha' la 'phros pa'i tshe/_/dngos grub lam dang bral ba yin/_/phyin cad mchod dang nus pa sbyin/_/rang gi skabs 
[\[193a.6\]]ni ma gtogs pa/_/gzhan du thig le mi ltung zhing /_/dbang sgyur lam la goms par bya/_/nyi zla 'dres pa'i bde ba nyid/_/zag med snying por thim pa'i tshe/_/sems kyi bde bar rang snang zhing /_/sems kyi bde ba bar chad med/_/gsal byed stobs dang bral ba'i tshe/_/
[\[193a.7\]]ma lus bde chen phyag rgya ste/_/lus sems mnyam du gnas pa nyid/_/tshad dang rgya dbyings ro las 'das/_/dpag med bde ba'i rol mo ni/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi dgyes chen te/_/thig le sa bon zag pa bral/_/bde chen nyams myong rgyun mi chad/_/rang nyid thig ler 
[\[193b\]]
[\[193b.1\]]nyams myong bas/_/snying po'i thig le bral ba med/_/phra zhing rdul bral dri ma med/_/rdo rje thig le dril bu nyid/_/'bad rtsol med par rang snang ba/_/bral thob med pa'i ngo bor gnas/_/gang du yon tan tshogs kyis rab rgyas pa/_/bde chen chu rgyun lta bu'i rgyun las byung /_/
[\[193b.2\]]tshogs gnyis yongs rdzogs bdag gzhan lhun gyis grub/_/gzhan na med de rang nyid snang bar zad/_/rang bzhin gnas phyir gzhan nas 'ong ba med/_/zhen pa bzlog pas bdag zhal bdag gis mthong /_/rdo rje sems dpa' sgyu 'phrul dra ba gsang ba me long gi rgyud chen 
[\[193b.3\]]po las lam gyi khyad par ma lus pa bshad pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o//_//de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba/_/ma lus kun gyi bsgrub bya nyid/_/phyogs bcu ma lus rdul snyed kyi/_/che ba'i yon tan bshad par bya/_/mthar phyin lnga yi rang bzhin ni/_/
[\[193b.4\]]rtogs dang 'bras bur gnas mod kyi/_/mthong dang goms dang grub pa nyid/_/thun mong 'bras bur yang dag bshad/_/yang dag mthar phyin 'bras bur ni/_/thabs chen thabs las byung ba yi/_/yid bzhin nor bu rin po che/_/dpag med yon tan lhun rdzogs 
[\[193b.5\]]pa/_/tshad dang dmigs pa las 'das pa/_/bsam yas lhun gyis grub pa ste/_/nam mkha'i mtha' ltar dpag med pa/_/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes ni/_/ma lus mchog gi gter gyur pa/_/zad 'phel med pa'i nor bu ste/_/ma lus kun la mnyam 
[\[193b.6\]]du gnas/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin ni/_/rgya dbyings tshad las 'das pa yi/_/dag dang ma dag ci snang yang /_/blang dor phyogs ris yongs kyis med/_/rang 'byung 'od gsal maN+Dal la/_/ma lus gcig gi rang bzhin te/_/thugs rje'i byin brlabs tshad med 
[\[193b.7\]]pas/_/ma lus bsam pa skong bar mdzad/_/rang byung ye shes lhun grub ni/_/chos rnams kun gyi ngo bo bas/_/ma lus phyogs med rang snang yang /_/zhen dang ched du byas pa med/_/sa dang lam la sogs pa yi/_/theg pa kun gyi 'bras bu ni/_/rang rang ltos pa'i 
[\[194a\]]
[\[194a.1\]]mthar thug kyang /_/che mchog lhun grub mnyam dang bral/_/skad cig nyid la lhun rdzogs pa'i/_/sangs rgyas dpag med yon tan rnams/_/rigs bdag rdo rje 'chang chen gyis/_/ye shes lha mo rol dbang gis/_/nyi zla'i bde ba mngon thob ste/_/rdo rje 
[\[194a.2\]]'chang gi sa la bkod/_/dpag med rgyal ba thams cad kyi/_/'gro ba'i gdul bya mtha' yas pas/_/sku gsung thugs kyang de bzhin te/_/bzhugs tshul dpag med rnam par snang /_/che ba'i yon tan lhun rdzogs pa'i/_/sangs rgyas drug pa'i rang bzhin te/_/nam 
[\[194a.3\]]mkha'i mthas gtugs mtshan pa yis/_/bde chen ye shes rgyas par gnas/_/bcu drug zhag gis rdzogs pa ltar/_/ma lus rdzogs pa'i ye shes nyid/_/sa mtha'i nyes pa kun bral ba/_/kun gyi yon tan bdag nyid gnas/_/sangs rgyas zhing khams mtha' 
[\[194a.4\]]yas pa'i/_/nam mkha'i mtha' thug bar du ni/_/gdul bya'i rdul snyed gnas kyi bar/_/skad cig 'du 'bral med par gnas/_/bcud 'dus thig le chen po ni/_/bde ba'i zag med thig le ste/_/a dang bde bar 'brel ba yi/_/ma lus khyab pa'i tshul du bzhugs/_/
[\[194a.5\]]thig le'i bye brag brgyad cu'i rtsa/_/bcud 'dus gsang ba'i thig le'i ro/_/ma skyes mnyam la gnas 'gyur zhing /_/ma lus gang ba'i tshul gyis ni/_/zhing khams phyogs bcu mtha' yas pa/_/zag med thig ler ro gcig pas/_/nam mkha'i gzugs can de bzhin 
[\[194a.6\]]nyid/_/sku bzhi 'dus pa'i khams thig le/_/dngas ma lnga 'dus snying po can/_/gsum 'dus mi 'gyur snying po ste/_/bdes gang bzhi bcu rtsa gnyis po/_/thig le ro yis gang phye ba/_/dpag med sku gsung yon tan thugs/_/bsam gyis mi khyab rang snang ba/_/thig 
[\[194a.7\]]le phyag rgya bsam mi khyab/_/phyi nang dngas snyigs ma lus pa/_/rang 'byung dngas ma'i thig le nyid/_/gzhan med phyogs med rang snang ba/_/phyogs med bde ba chen po nyid/_/gsang ba'i thig le chen po ni/_/yon tan rdzogs pa'i ngo bo ste/_/bde ba chen po'i snying po las/_/
[\[194b\]]
[\[194b.1\]]dpag med yon tan rnam par 'phrul/_/bde ba chen po'i thig le ni/_/'du 'phro brel bas zag pa med/_/phyogs ris 'das pas ma khyab med/_/thog mtha' med par mnyam pa yin/_/rgyal ba kun gyi rang bzhin te/_/thig le ro la mnyam bzhugs pas/_/nam mkha'i 
[\[194b.2\]]mthas gtugs chos thams cad/_/bde ba chen por gsal bar myong /_/mtshan dang dpe byad tshad 'das pa/_/rang la rdzogs pa'i thig le nyid/_/ji snyed bde ba'i rol pa kun/_/rang la rdzogs pa nyid du gnas/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi bde ba ni/_/rang 'byung thig le chen po ste/_/
[\[194b.3\]]A li kA li zhu ba las/_/nam mkha' khyab pa'i bde ba 'byung /_/gsang ba'i thig le kun gyi rgyu/_/nam mkha'i thig le gsang ba'i ngang /_/khyab pa chen po phyogs cha med/_/lus gnas rang bzhin ma skyes pa/_/bde ba'i dbang phyug sku gsung thugs/_/nam mkha' lta 
[\[194b.4\]]bur dpag tu med/_/thabs dang shes rab dbyer med pa'i/_/gsang ba'i thig le bzhugs pa'i gnas/_/dpag med til 'bru rdul snyed kyi/_/rgyal ba kun dang lhan cig tu/_/ma lus bde ba chen po'i ro/_/lhan cig skyes pa'i rang bzhin gyis/_/ma gsungs rig pas nyams 
[\[194b.5\]]btang bas/_/mi 'dzin longs spyod chen po'i gnas/_/rang 'byung nyi zla rten gyi ro/_/ma spangs 'dus pas 'du 'bral med/_/gsang ba'i thig le bcud 'dus pa/_/nam mkha' gang ba bde ba'i ro/_/gnyis med 'khril ba'i stobs las byung /_/mkha' dbyings byang chub 
[\[194b.6\]]'byor pa'i sems/_/ma btang ma spros thig le che/_/nam mkha' rgyas pa'i mtha' bzhin du/_/bde ba'i tshad kyang dpag tu med/_/yab yum gnas pa'i grangs tshad kyang /_/nam mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam/_khams kyi rdul snyed de/_/re re'ang bde ba'i dkyil 'khor ni/_/rdul snyed nam 
[\[194b.7\]]mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam/_/yab yum gnyis med 'brel pa yi/_/thabs dang shes rab bcud 'dus pa/_/gang yang mi sgrib thig le'i ro/_/snang gsum mtshan ma las 'das pa/_/sangs rgyas rin chen kun gyi bcud/_/bye ba rdul snyed grangs 'das pa'i/_/ma lus kun dang 
[\[195a\]]
[\[195a.1\]]lhan cig tu/_/mos dang chags pa'i khyad par gyis/_/cha lugs mtha' yas snang ba ni/_/la lar chags par lta ba'i tshul/_/de bzhin 'jam par smra ba dang /_/la lar 'khyud cing lta ba ste/_/la lar sen thabs zhal sbyor zhing /_/la lar mnye 
[\[195a.2\]]dang rdebs dang 'bud/_/'gyed dang 'dud dang 'gying ba yi/_/dkar 'dzin ston dang lte ba ston/_/'o dang 'tshir dang dgod pa dag_/sna tshogs rol pa 'di dag gis/_/ma chags pa dag chags byed cing /_/chags pa myur du nyams par byed/_/tha skam myur du bengs byed 
[\[195a.3\]]cing /_/bde chen lo tog skyed par byed/_/bye ba rdul snyed bcas pa yi/_/lha dang lha mo 'khor bcas pas/_/steg cing 'dzum pas lhan cig tu/_/rol cing glu ni len par byed/_/e ma'o sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin bde ba che/_/lhan cig skyes pa mkha' ltar 
[\[195a.4\]]rdul dang bral ba ste/_/rdo rje pad+mar bcug bskyod pas ni brtags pa'i snang ba nub/_/ma lus khyab pas mkha' ltar dga' bar byas kyang dbang po rlung gis rlabs/_/bde ba'i sa bon zag pa rnams ni slar yang ldang bar byed/_/rgyu ba rnam par chad pa'i kha sbyor bde chen 
[\[195a.5\]]ni/_/nam mkha'i rgya mnyam yun yang de srid bar du gnas pa ste/_/'dod pa'i yon tan lnga ni lha mo khyod la tshang ba ste/_/bdag ni khyod la bsten cing dga' bas mig bteg ma lus rgyal ba'i zhing du blta/_/gang tshe 'gro ba tha mal bder spyod yul la chags par zhugs 
[\[195a.6\]]pa na/_/dga' dang bde dang rtse gcig yid 'ong 'dod par rab zhugs pas/_/dbang po gnyis rol nyi zla phrad cing bde chen mchog skyes che/_/skye rga na 'chi re dogs ngo tsha mi tsha grags pa'i chos brgyad tshod kyang byis pas mi shes so/_/gang zhig thabs dang shes rab 
[\[195a.7\]]bral bas gsang ba'i don 'jug pa/_/rgya mtsho la ni sa bon btab bzhin 'dod pa'i 'bras bu mi 'byung ste/_/nyan thos rang rgyal byang chub sems dpa' tha skabs te/_/rgyal ba nyid kyang thabs dang shes rab bral bas byang chub myu gu gtan nas skams to/_/'di ni glu 
[\[195b\]]
[\[195b.1\]]ru blangs pa yis/_/rdul snyed bcas pa'i 'khor tshogs rnams/_/rang rang rgyan dang gos dor te/_/dpa' bos sgeg mo'i tshul 'dzin cing /_/dkyil 'khor phyogs bcu'i rdul snyed du/_/glu dang gar ni sna tshogs kyis/_/tha mal yid rtogs bcad nas ni/_/bde chen 
[\[195b.2\]]yid ni 'phrog par mdzad/_/de bzhin dkyil 'khor bsam yas dang /_/zhing thabs bde ba rmad 'byung ba/_/'dul ba'i thabs ni sna tshogs pa/_/dpag med mtha' mas cir snang yang /_/rang byung ye shes byang chub sems/_/thig le gcig gi ngo bo las/_/
[\[195b.3\]]mdzad pa'i gar thabs sna tshogs kyis/_/'dul ba'i dbang du ci yang spro/_/thabs la thabs kyis dbang sgyur bas/_/thabs chen thabs las byung ba yi/_/ngo mtshar ya mtshan rmad po che/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin te/_/thabs chen sgyu ma rmad du byung /_/
[\[195b.4\]]sgyu ma skye 'gag med pa'i dbyings/_/sku gsung yon tan phrin las thugs/_/ma lus kun 'dus rnam par dag_/thams cad rang rig byang chub sems/_/kun nas 'khyil ba'i dkyil 'khor te/_/kun gyi rang bzhin 'byung gnas nyid/_/bya ba byed la byas pa med/_/zad 
[\[195b.5\]]dang 'phel ba med pa yi/_/dngos grub gter chen bsam mi khyab/_/ye shes thig le rang snang ba/_/bsam gyis mi khyab mtha' yas mchog_/mtha' dbus med pa'i ye shes ni/_/gcig ces 'di zhes brjod du med/_/nam mkha' rang 'byung rtag pa ltar/_/'pho 
[\[195b.6\]]'gyur med pas de bzhin no/_/nam mkha' mtha' dbus med pa bzhin/_/rang 'byung ye shes thog mtha' med/_/ma byung ma skyes byang chub sems/_/ye shes nyi ma'i 'od ltar gsal/_/sangs rgyas sems can grol thar kun/_/ma lus rang rig 'od gsal bas/_/
[\[195b.7\]]rang 'byung ye shes rang snang phyir/_/mkhyen dang bsgribs pa [{]chung zad [,]cung zad [}]med/_/mkhyen pa'i ye shes nyag gcig la/_/rang gi cha shas chos bral bas/_/nam mkha'i mtha' thug chos rnams kun/_/skad cig ma 'dres tshul du mkhyen/_/rang rig byang chub sems nyid 
[\[196a\]]
[\[196a.1\]]las/_/mtho dman blang dor ma dmigs pas/_/'khor ba'i ngo bor rang snang yang /_/ma chags skyon med pad+ma 'dra/_/dpag med yon tan ngo bo rnams/_/rang 'byung rig pa rang snang bas/_/ma lus ngo mtshar rgya mtsho rnams/_/rang snang gzhan las 
[\[196a.2\]]thob bya med/_/byin brlabs cho 'phrul rmad kyi chos/_/rang 'byung nyid las rnam par spro/_/tshul ni rnam pa sna tshogs te/_/dag dang ma dag khyad par gyis/_/ngo bo gcig las ma g.yos kyang /_/mos pas mthong tshul de bzhin no/_/gsung ni nam mkha' khyab par ni/_/phyogs 
[\[196a.3\]]bcu khyab par sgra sgrogs pa/_/mos pa bzhin du sna tshogs pa'i/_/bsam yas 'dul thabs sna tshogs snang /_/thugs ni ma lus mkhyen pa'i thugs/_/phan dang bder bcas brtser ldan pa'i/_/mngon shes mkhyen pa'i ye shes spro/_/ye shes thugs la khyad par med/_/
[\[196a.4\]]yon tan tshad khyad bral ba ste/_/nam mkha'i nor bu gter 'chang ba/_/dpag med bsam pa bskang ba'i phyir/_/byin brlabs yid bzhin nor bu'i tshul/_/phrin las byin brlabs rang snang ba/_/'gro 'ong med pa'i don tshul gyis/_/dpag bsam bum dang rin chen gyi/_/bya byed 
[\[196a.5\]]med par don rnams rdzogs/_/ma lus spros pa rnam dag cing /_/rang 'byung rig pa mtha' dbus med/_/ma lus kun gyi 'byung gnas te/_/bde ba chen po chos kyi sku/_/'dod pa'i longs spyod thams cad ni/_/gnyis med ngang la kun rdzogs pas/_/de nyid tshad bral mnga' 
[\[196a.6\]]brnyes pas/_/longs spyod yongs su rdzogs pa'i sku/_/sku gcig thig le'i ngo bo las/_/'jig rten khams ni thams cad du/_/ji ltar 'dul ba'i tshul bzhin du/_/rnam par 'phrul pa sprul pa'i sku/_/ma byung ma skyes don rtogs pas/_/ma lus dbyer med rdo rje nyid/_/mi 
[\[196a.7\]]'jigs gnyen po kun bral ba/_/rdo rje sku zhes rnam par bstan/_/gang du mngon par byang chub pa'i/_/'od gsal nam mkha'i mtha' dbus med/_/sku bzhi mtha' yis ma reg pa/_/dang po mngon par byang chub pa'o/_/ma lus dbye ba mtha' spangs shing /_/de ltar kun dang 
[\[196b\]]
[\[196b.1\]]mnyam par gnas/_/don gyis ma lus khyab 'gyur ba/_/brjod med don gyi gsung nyid do/_/sku dang phyag rgya gzigs tshul gyis/_/ting 'dzin dgongs pas 'dul ba la/_/brjod med tshul gyis gang ston pa/_/brda yi gsung du rnam par bstan/_/gang yang rung ba mthong 
[\[196b.2\]]thos tshe/_/dpa' bos bltas pas g.yul sel ltar/_/dngos pos byang chub nam mkhar rtogs/_/byin brlabs rtogs pa'i gsung zhes bya/_/gang gi don du gang snang ba/_/gnyen po bar chad mi gnas shing /_/gzhan dag pham byed mi thub pa/_/zil gyis gnon 
[\[196b.3\]]pa'i gsung du brjod/_/tshig gcig brjod pas tshig mtha' yas/_/mos pa de rnyed rnam grol cing /_/yon tan rgya mtsho phun sum tshogs/_/'di ni brjod pa tshig gi gsung /_/ji snyed mang por rnam spros kyang /_/chos kyi dbyings la khyad par med/_/rtog la rnam rtog mtshan 
[\[196b.4\]]ma yang /_/mi rtog ye shes grol ba'i thugs/_/gnyis med bde ba chen po yis/_/'dod pa'i longs spyod lnga la rol/_/ma lus bde bar snang ba'i tshe/_/'di ni bde ba chen po'i thugs/_/bdag gzhan phyi nang mi mnga' yang /_/rang 'byung gnyis kyi phyogs bral bas/_/
[\[196b.5\]]gdul bya'i bsam pa kun mkhyen pas/_/'gro ba ma lus bsgral ba'i thugs/_/ji snyed rig pa'i don snang zhing /_/ma lus kun gyi ngo bo la/_/skye 'jig gnyen po gdod nas stong /_/mi 'gyur rdo rje thugs zhes bya/_/dpag med nam mkha'i zhing khams dang /_/
[\[196b.6\]]che ba'i khyad par bstan pa'i gdan/_/zag pa med pa'i rgyan rnams dang /_/ye shes 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang /_/mtha' dbus med par khyab pa yi/_/'od zer tshogs ni rnam par snang /_/gzhan yang yon tan chen po ni/_/mtha' dbus med pa'i nam mkha' ltar/_/
[\[196b.7\]]dpag cing gzhal bar mi nus te/_/zag med che ba'i yon tan snang /_/gzhan yang mdzad pa'i phrin las ni/_/gdul bya'i yul ni ji snyed pa/_/mdzad pa'i sgo ni bsam yas te/_/mtha' thug med par rnam par snang /_/'di dag bsdus pas bshad bya na/_/zhi rgyas 
[\[197a\]]
[\[197a.1\]]dbang drag la sogs pas/_/mtha' yas bzhis ni rnam par bsdus/_/lhun gyis grub par kun kyang 'grub/_/mdor na 'bras bu'i rnam grangs ni/_/bsdus pas nyi shu rtsa lngar gnas/_/rdo rje sems dpa' sgyu 'phrul dra ba gsang ba me long gi rgyud 
[\[197a.2\]]chen po las lhun gyis grub pa ma lus pa bshad pa'i le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o//_//de nas gsang bdag rdo rje 'dzin/_/dpag med rgyal bas dbang bskur ba/_/gsang ba'i bdag po sngags kyi mdzod/_/gter 'dzin rgyal po stobs po che/_/rang gi lag gi 
[\[197a.3\]]rdo rje nyid/_/dga' zhing mgu bas gsor byas te/_/bye ba rdul snyed bcas pa yis/_/rab tu bskor ba byas pa yis/_/'jig rten drug tu bcas pa dag_/'khrugs shing rnam par g.yo mdzad de/_/kun du rab g.yo rnam par g.yo/_/stong gsum 'jig rten 'khor 
[\[197a.4\]]yug bcas/_/'od dang sgra yis gang mdzad de/_/rmad 'byung sku gsung thugs rnams kyi/_/ngo mtshar cho 'phrul rnam par snang /_/ngo mtshar cho 'phrul rmad 'byung gi/_/phyi nang gsang ba'i mchod pa dang /_/'dod yon lnga yis khyab mdzad de/_/rgyal ba'i tshogs 
[\[197a.5\]]kun mnyes par byas/_/kun kyang dga' zhing mos pa yis/_/zhing khams rdul dang mnyam pa yi/_/'khor tshogs rnams ni bstod pa 'di/_/sna tshogs sgras ni glu ru blangs/_/o~M AHhU~M a la la ho/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i thugs ni mi skye ye shes bde ba'i thugs/_/mi 
[\[197a.6\]]rtogs gsal ba'i thugs ni kun la khyab par gnas/_/gang yang dbyer med 'dus byas ma yin 'dus ma byas min skye 'jig med/_/dam pa'i thugs la byung tshor g.yo 'gog ci yang med pas bde ho/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i sku ni sna tshogs 'od 'bar bde ba'i sku/_/sna tshogs 
[\[197a.7\]]mtha' yas ji ltar snang ba ngo bos dben/_/de nyid 'du ba'i thabs kyis de ltar yid 'phrog tshim pa'i sku/_/mtha' yas sna tshogs bye brag ji ltar gsal yang nam mkha'i ngang du bye brag med ho/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i yon tan yid bzhin nor bu rin po che/_/mi zad par ldan 
[\[197b\]]
[\[197b.1\]]rgyan gyi 'khor lo dpag med rgyas par snang /_/'gro ba rnams ni yid 'phrog bltas pas chog shes med par mdzes/_/rgyal ba ma lus yon tan rdzogs par snang la de dag mtha' thug med ho/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i gsung ni zab cing rgya cher nam mkha' 'dra/_/ma lus go 
[\[197b.2\]]zhing tshim mdzad rdo rje'i sgra 'byin snyan sgrogs pa/_/rdo rje sgra chen mtha' yas mos dang 'thad pa de bzhin rnam par snang ho/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i phrin las sna tshogs bya ba lhun gyis grub/_/bskal pa zhing gi rdul snyed 'gro ba ma lus kun 'dul ba/_/thabs dang shes rab 'du 'bral 
[\[197b.3\]]med pa'i rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs 'di ni rnam par rol ho/_/de nas rgyal mchog skyes bu yis/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa la sogs pa/_/bye ba rdul snyed bcas pa la/_/gsang ba 'di ni gtad par mdzad/_/nga yi gsang ba'i snying po 'di/_/dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/_/bstan pa 
[\[197b.4\]]kun gyi rang bzhin te/_/ma lus kun gyi thabs su snang /_/gsang sngags rdo rje theg pa ste/_/dpal ldan sgyu 'phrul thugs kyi rgyud/_/chos rnams ma lus kun snang ba/_/gsang ba'i me long thabs kyi rgyud/_/rgyal ba rdul snyed kun 'dus nas/_/dam pa 'di la bsngags par 
[\[197b.5\]]mdzad/_/sgyu 'phrul dra ba'i rgyud chen po/_/zab mo'i lung gi nges don 'di/_/rigs chad dman pa'i gang zag gis/_/thams cad la ni me long ltar/_/bltas kyang gzugs brnyan mi snang bas/_/ston pa gnyis la rgyab phyogs shing /_/brtags pa'i phyi rol chos la zhen/_/
[\[197b.6\]]slob dpon la ni brnyas byed dang /_/rdo rje spun la sdang sems che/_/zab mo'i don la skur byed cing /_/gsang rgyud don la 'phya ba dang /_/ser sna rang phyogs chags pa che/_/dad dang dam tshig mi ldan zhing /_/rgyal srid rin chen mi gtong ba'i/_/phyi nang gnyis lta'i gang zag la/_/
[\[197b.7\]]bskal pa zhing gi rdul snyed du/_/lus sems bral bar byed gyur kyang /_/gsang ba'i glegs bam mi bstan na/_/dbang dang dngos grub nams kyang min/_/rmongs pas gal te byin na ni/_/de nyid mnar med dmyal bar ltung /_/dad dang dam tshig ldan pa yis/_/phun 
[\[198a\]]
[\[198a.1\]]sum tshogs pa kun gtong zhing /_/dam pa'i yo byad ldan gyur pas/_/lus dang srog la'ang mi lta na/_/dman pa'i longs spyod nams kyang min/_/theg pa che la mos pa dang /_/brtul zhugs snying brtser gus par ldan/_/rdo rje slob dpon bsnyen 
[\[198a.2\]]bkur zhing /_/skyon dang spyo ba yongs su bzod/_/bskal par 'grogs kyang yid mi 'khrug_/nyes dang legs pa ci la yang /_/ngo mtshar skyo ba'i 'du shes med/_/rang bzhin bzang dang tshul shes dang /_/'grogs pas dug nad mi ldang ba'i/_/dam pa 'di la gsang 
[\[198a.3\]]ba gtang /_/slob dpon man ngag mi ldan cing /_/gsang ba'i don la ma goms pas/_/rang rtags log pa'i tshad ma yis/_/gsang ba'i sbas don gtan la 'bebs/_/blun po mun pas chod pa rnams/_/log par zhen pa'i rjes 'brangs nas/_/mthong ba'i chos 
[\[198a.4\]]la dmyal bar smin/_/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi/_/phyag rgya gsang chen snying po 'di/_/ma lus kun gyi gsang yin pas/_/rtogs nas gsal bar gang byed pa/_/dbang dang dkyil 'khor rdzogs pa ste/_/bdag dang gzhan don lhun gyis grub/_/rdul snyed 
[\[198a.5\]]zhing la bsnyen bkur zhing /_/tshogs kyi bla ma bsten byas pa'i/_/'bras bu gsang chen 'di yin te/_/de bas rgyal sras gcig gi yul/_/rnyed dang bkur stis 'tsho ba yi/_/grags pa'i stobs ldan rnam par spangs/_/a ho rdo rje gsang ba'i tshig tu'o/_/rdo rje sems dpa'i sgyu 
[\[198a.6\]]'phrul dra ba gsang ba me long gi rgyud las mthar phyin pa'i le'u ste bcu gsum pa'o//_//rdo rje sems dpa'i sgyu 'phrul dra ba rdo rje sems dpa' gsang ba me long gi rgyud chen po rdzogs [{]s+ho[,]so[}]//_//rgya gar gyi mkhan po bi ma la 
[\[198a.7\]]mi tra dang /_ban+de dz+nyA na ku mA ras bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa/_/slad nas bi ma la mi tra nyid la ze mo dge slong mas yang dang yang du zhus pa 'di gzhan la spel na dam tshig nyams so//_//
